<!-- Wistia standard player rollout: Wistia providers only. -->
<script id="varonis-wistia-standard">
(() => {
  if (window.__varonisWistiaStandardReady) return;
  window.__varonisWistiaStandardReady = true;
  const dataScripts = new Set();
  function media(value) {
    try {
      const url = new URL(value, location.href);
      if (url.protocol !== 'https:') return null;
      if (url.hostname === 'cdn.embedly.com') {
        return media(url.searchParams.get('src') || url.searchParams.get('url') || '');
      }
      if (!/(^|\.)(wistia\.com|wistia\.net)$/.test(url.hostname)) return null;
      const id = url.pathname.match(/\/(?:medias|iframe)\/([a-zA-Z0-9]{10})(?:\/|$)/)?.[1];
      return id ? {id, url} : null;
    } catch (_) { return null; }
  }
  function data(id) {
    if (dataScripts.has(id)) return;
    dataScripts.add(id);
    const source = 'https://fast.wistia.com/embed/medias/' + id + '.jsonp';
    if (![...document.scripts].some(element => element.src === source)) {
      const script = document.createElement('script');
      script.async = true;
      script.src = source;
      script.dataset.varonisWistiaData = id;
      document.head.append(script);
    }
    const playerSource = 'https://fast.wistia.com/assets/external/E-v1.js';
    if (![...document.scripts].some(element => element.src === playerSource)) {
      const playerScript = document.createElement('script');
      playerScript.async = true;
      playerScript.src = playerSource;
      document.head.append(playerScript);
    }
  }
  function mount(frame, info, title, flexible) {
    if (!frame?.isConnected || frame.dataset.varonisWistiaMedia) return;
    const player = document.createElement('div');
    for (const attribute of frame.attributes) {
      if (attribute.name === 'class' || attribute.name === 'id' || attribute.name === 'style' ||
          attribute.name === 'hidden' || attribute.name === 'aria-label' ||
          (attribute.name.startsWith('data-') && !attribute.name.startsWith('data-consent'))) {
        player.setAttribute(attribute.name, attribute.value);
      }
    }
    player.classList.remove('wistia_embed_initialized');
    player.classList.add('wistia_embed', 'wistia_async_' + info.id);
    const options = new Map();
    info.url.searchParams.forEach((value, key) => {
      if (/^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9_[\]]*$/.test(key) && key !== 'web_component') options.set(key, value);
    });
    if (!options.has('videoFoam')) options.set('videoFoam', 'true');
    options.set('doNotTrack', 'true');
    options.set('resumable', 'false');
    options.forEach((value, key) => {
      for (const name of [...player.classList]) if (name.startsWith(key + '=')) player.classList.remove(name);
      player.classList.add(key + '=' + encodeURIComponent(value));
    });
    player.dataset.varonisWistiaMedia = info.id;
    player.setAttribute('aria-label', title || frame.getAttribute('title') || 'Video');
    const style = getComputedStyle(frame);
    if (flexible || frame.closest('.w-video,.wistia_responsive_wrapper') || style.position === 'absolute') {
      player.style.width = '100%';
      player.style.height = '100%';
      if (!flexible || style.position === 'absolute') {
        player.style.position = 'absolute';
        player.style.inset = '0';
      }
    } else {
      const width = frame.getAttribute('width');
      const height = frame.getAttribute('height');
      if (!frame.style.width) player.style.width = /^\d+$/.test(width || '') ? width + 'px' : '100%';
      if (!frame.style.height && /^\d+$/.test(height || '')) player.style.height = height + 'px';
      player.style.maxWidth = '100%';
    }
    player.style.border = '0';
    player.style.display = flexible ? 'block' : style.display === 'none' ? 'none' : 'block';
    frame.replaceWith(player);
    data(info.id);
  }
  function flexible(root) {
    if (root.querySelector('[data-varonis-wistia-media]')) return;
    const config = key => root.querySelector('[data-flexible-media-config="' + key + '"]')?.textContent.trim() || '';
    if (config('type').toLowerCase() !== 'video') return;
    const info = media(config('embed') || root.querySelector('video')?.getAttribute('src') || '');
    if (!info) return;
    const frame = root.querySelector('.flexible-media_embed');
    if (!frame) return;
    root.dataset.mediaExtension = 'true';
    const height = config('height');
    if (height && height !== 'auto' && CSS.supports('height', height)) {
      [root, root.querySelector('.flexible-media_frame'), root.closest('.product-hero_media,.centered-switchback_media,.switchback_media')]
        .filter(Boolean).forEach(element => element.style.height = height);
    }
    root.querySelectorAll('.flexible-media_image,.flexible-media_video,.flexible-media_rive,.flexible-media_renderer')
      .forEach(element => { element.style.display = 'none'; });
    const video = root.querySelector('video');
    video?.pause();
    video?.removeAttribute('src');
    mount(frame, info, config('title'), true);
  }
  function iframe(frame) {
    const info = media(frame.getAttribute('fs-consent-src') || frame.getAttribute('src') || frame.getAttribute('data-src') || '');
    if (info) mount(frame, info);
  }
  function scan(root) {
    if (root.nodeType !== 1 && root.nodeType !== 9) return;
    const parent = root.closest?.('.flexible-media');
    if (parent) flexible(parent);
    if (root.matches?.('.flexible-media')) flexible(root);
    root.querySelectorAll('.flexible-media').forEach(flexible);
    if (root.matches?.('iframe')) iframe(root);
    root.querySelectorAll('iframe').forEach(iframe);
  }
  const pending = new Set();
  let queued = false;
  const observer = new MutationObserver(records => {
    for (const record of records) {
      if (record.type === 'attributes') pending.add(record.target);
      else {
        for (const node of record.addedNodes) if (node.nodeType === 1) pending.add(node);
        if (record.target.closest?.('[data-flexible-media-config]')) pending.add(record.target.closest('.flexible-media'));
      }
    }
    if (queued || !pending.size) return;
    queued = true;
    queueMicrotask(() => {
      queued = false;
      const roots = [...pending]; pending.clear();
      roots.forEach(root => { if (root?.isConnected) scan(root); });
    });
  });
  const start = () => {
    scan(document);
    observer.observe(document.documentElement, {
      subtree: true, childList: true, attributes: true,
      attributeFilter: ['src', 'fs-consent-src', 'data-src']
    });
  };
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', start, {once:true});
  else start();
})();
</script>

<script>
(() => {
  function syncOverviewLink() {
    const desktop = document.querySelector('a[data-native-origin="8b86be46-a9de-6332-6edb-b4c57cd98d8a"]');
    const mobile = document.querySelector('a[data-native-origin="8b86be46-a9de-6332-6edb-b4c57cd98ec2"]');
    if (!desktop?.hasAttribute('href') || !mobile) return;
    mobile.setAttribute('href', desktop.getAttribute('href'));
    if (desktop.getAttribute('aria-current') === 'page') {
      mobile.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
      mobile.classList.add('w--current');
    }
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncOverviewLink, { once: true });
  } else {
    syncOverviewLink();
  }
})();

 let open=false,lockedY=0,bodyStyle=null;const mq=matchMedia('(max-width:1023px)'),siblings=[];for(let branch=header.closest('.nav')||header;branch&&branch!==root;branch=branch.parentElement){const parent=branch.parentElement;if(!parent)break;for(const el of parent.children)if(el!==branch&&!['SCRIPT','STYLE','LINK'].includes(el.tagName))siblings.push(el)};const inertBefore=new Map();
 const gradient=()=>nav.toggleAttribute('data-scroll-more',scroll.scrollHeight-scroll.clientHeight-scroll.scrollTop>2);
 const sections=()=>{nav.toggleAttribute('data-section-open',!!scroll.querySelector('details[data-mobile-expanded="true"]'));requestAnimationFrame(gradient);};

 // Preserve native summaries and keep closing content painted only for the CSS exit.

  const summary=item.querySelector(':scope > summary'),panel=item.querySelector(':scope > .nav-panel');if(!summary||!panel)return;

  let expanded=item.open,timer=0;item.dataset.mobileExpanded=String(expanded);grid.inert=!expanded;grid.setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(!expanded));summary.setAttribute('aria-controls',grid.id);summary.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(expanded));
  const finish=()=>{if(!expanded)item.open=false;clearTimeout(timer);gradient();};
  const set=(next)=>{if(next===expanded)return;clearTimeout(timer);expanded=next;
   if(!next&&grid.contains(document.activeElement))summary.focus({preventScroll:true});

  disclosureMotion.addEventListener('change',()=>{if(disclosureMotion.matches)finish();});

<script>
/* Webflow Cloud dark-navigation motion adapted to native Webflow dropdowns.
Native Webflow owns opening, dismissal, keyboard navigation and link targets. */
(() => {
function init() {
const header=document.querySelector('body[data-vrn-chrome] .site-header');
if(!header || header.dataset.darkNavMotion) return;
header.dataset.darkNavMotion='true';
const nav=header.querySelector('.desktop-nav');
if(!nav) return;
const menus=[...nav.querySelectorAll(':scope > .w-dropdown')];
const buttons=[...nav.querySelectorAll(':scope > a, :scope > .w-dropdown > .w-dropdown-toggle')];
const desktop=matchMedia('(min-width:1024px)'), reduced=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce)');
const dark=()=>true; // Both themes share navigation layout and motion.
const open=m=>m.querySelector(':scope > .w-dropdown-toggle').classList.contains('w--open');
const highlight=document.createElement('span');highlight.className='nav-tab-highlight';highlight.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');nav.prepend(highlight);
let hovered=null,frame,previous=null,bounds=null,closedAt=-Infinity,leaveTimer,sampleFrame,snapshot=null;
const finishes=new Set();
const clear=()=>{for(const f of [...finishes])f()};
function updateHighlight(){
const button=hovered||buttons.find(b=>b===document.activeElement&&b.matches(':focus-visible'))||buttons.find(b=>b.classList.contains('w--open'));
if(!dark()||!desktop.matches||!button){highlight.removeAttribute('data-visible');return}
const r=button.getBoundingClientRect(),origin=header.getBoundingClientRect(),entering=!highlight.hasAttribute('data-visible');
if(entering)highlight.style.transition='none';
Object.assign(highlight.style,{transform:`translate(${r.left-origin.left-header.clientLeft}px,${r.top-origin.top-header.clientTop}px)`,width:r.width+'px',height:r.height+'px'});
if(entering){highlight.getBoundingClientRect();highlight.style.removeProperty('transition')}
highlight.setAttribute('data-visible','');
}
const schedule=()=>{cancelAnimationFrame(frame);frame=requestAnimationFrame(updateHighlight)};
// Keep the last rendered composition, including interrupted outgoing content.
function capture(){
if(!previous||!open(previous))return;
const panel=previous.querySelector('.nav-panel'),r=panel.getBoundingClientRect();
if(!r.height)return;
snapshot={menu:previous,width:r.width,height:r.height,layers:[...panel.children].filter(e=>e.matches('.nav-panel-columns,.nav-motion-outgoing')).map(e=>({node:e,width:e.getBoundingClientRect().width,opacity:getComputedStyle(e).opacity,transform:getComputedStyle(e).transform}))};
}
function sample(){capture();sampleFrame=requestAnimationFrame(sample);}
function animate(menu){
const panel=menu.querySelector('.nav-panel'),content=panel?.querySelector('.nav-panel-columns');if(!content)return;
capture();const saved=snapshot?{...snapshot,layers:snapshot.layers.map(layer=>({...layer,node:layer.node.cloneNode(true)}))}:null;
const switching=saved&&(hovered||performance.now()-closedAt<300);
cancelAnimationFrame(sampleFrame);clear();
const next=panel.getBoundingClientRect();previous=menu;bounds=next;closedAt=Infinity;
if(!desktop.matches||reduced.matches){snapshot=null;return;}
const animations=[],outgoing=[];let done=false;
// Freeze each content grid at its own final width while the panel resizes.
const contentWidth=content.getBoundingClientRect().width,priorWidth=content.style.width,priorMinWidth=content.style.minWidth;
content.style.width=contentWidth+'px';content.style.minWidth=contentWidth+'px';
const finish=()=>{if(done)return;done=true;animations.forEach(a=>a.cancel());outgoing.forEach(e=>e.remove());content.style.width=priorWidth;content.style.minWidth=priorMinWidth;panel.classList.remove('nav-panel-animating');finishes.delete(finish);};
finishes.add(finish);panel.classList.add('nav-panel-animating');
const timing={duration:250,easing:'cubic-bezier(0,0,.2,1)',fill:'both'};
if(switching){
const direction=menus.indexOf(menu)>=menus.indexOf(saved.menu)?1:-1;
for(const layer of saved.layers){
const ghost=layer.node;ghost.style.width=layer.width+'px';ghost.style.minWidth=layer.width+'px';ghost.style.right='auto';ghost.classList.add('nav-motion-outgoing');ghost.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');ghost.inert=true;ghost.removeAttribute('id');ghost.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(e=>e.removeAttribute('id'));panel.append(ghost);outgoing.push(ghost);
animations.push(ghost.animate([{opacity:layer.opacity,transform:layer.transform},{opacity:0,transform:`translateX(${-direction*40}px)`}],timing));
}
animations.push(content.animate([{opacity:0,transform:`translateX(${direction*40}px)`},{opacity:1,transform:'translateX(0)'}],timing));
animations.push(panel.animate([{height:saved.height+'px',width:saved.width+'px'},{height:next.height+'px',width:next.width+'px'}],timing));
}else animations.push(panel.animate([{opacity:0},{opacity:1}],{duration:160,easing:'ease-out'}));
sample();
Promise.all(animations.map(a=>a.finished.catch(()=>{}))).then(()=>{if(done)return;finish();cancelAnimationFrame(sampleFrame);capture();});
}
let states=menus.map(open);
new MutationObserver(()=>{
const next=menus.map(open);
menus.forEach((m,i)=>{if(states[i]&&!next[i]&&previous===m)closedAt=performance.now()});
menus.forEach((m,i)=>{if(!states[i]&&next[i])animate(m)});
states=next;schedule();
}).observe(nav,{attributes:true,subtree:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
buttons.forEach(button=>{
button.addEventListener('pointerenter',e=>{if(e.pointerType==='touch')return;clearTimeout(leaveTimer);hovered=button;updateHighlight()});
button.addEventListener('pointerleave',()=>{clearTimeout(leaveTimer);leaveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(hovered===button)hovered=null;schedule();},100)});
});
nav.addEventListener('focusin',schedule);nav.addEventListener('focusout',schedule);
const reset=()=>{clearTimeout(leaveTimer);cancelAnimationFrame(sampleFrame);snapshot=null;clear();previous=null;bounds=null;closedAt=-Infinity;hovered=null;schedule()};
function syncTheme(){nav.classList.toggle('nav-glide-ready',dark());reset()}
new MutationObserver(syncTheme).observe(header,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['data-nav-state','class']});
desktop.addEventListener('change',reset);reduced.addEventListener('change',reset);window.addEventListener('resize',reset);document.fonts.ready.then(schedule);syncTheme();
}
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();

</script>
<!-- Shared hero clearance follows the fixed announcement and navigation. -->

<script id="site-hero-clearance-controller">
(()=>{function init(){
 const root=document.querySelector('body[data-vrn-chrome]');
 const header=root?.querySelector('.site-header');
 const shell=root?.querySelector('.site-nav-shell');
 const bar=root?.querySelector('.announcement-bar');
 if(!root||!header||root.dataset.heroClearanceReady)return;
 root.dataset.heroClearanceReady='true';
 let frame;
 const sync=()=>{frame=0;const nav=header.getBoundingClientRect();
  const announcement=bar&&!bar.hidden?bar.getBoundingClientRect().bottom:0;
  root.style.setProperty('--site-chrome-bottom',Math.ceil(Math.max(nav.bottom,announcement,0))+'px');
 };
 const schedule=()=>{if(!frame)frame=requestAnimationFrame(sync)};
 const observer=new ResizeObserver(schedule);
 [header,shell,bar].filter(Boolean).forEach(el=>observer.observe(el));
 if(bar)new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(bar,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['hidden','style','class']});
 window.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
 document.fonts?.ready.then(schedule);
 sync();schedule();
 }document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true}):init();
})();

varying vec2 vUv;
uniform float uTime,uHorizon,uMouseRadius,uMouseBoost;
uniform vec2 uRes,uMouse;
void main(){
 vec2 p=vec2((vUv.x-.5)*uRes.x/uRes.y,vUv.y);
 float h=uHorizon-p.x*p.x*.17;
 float d=p.y-h;
 float rimDistance=p.y-(uHorizon-p.x*p.x*.29);
 float pulse=.96+.04*sin(uTime*.85);
 float envelope=exp(-p.x*p.x*.85);
 float tight=exp(-pow(rimDistance/.028,2.))*envelope;
 float halo=exp(-pow(rimDistance/.065,2.))*exp(-p.x*p.x*1.7);
 float upper=exp(-max(-d,0.)*.55)*(1.-smoothstep(-.025,.06,d))*exp(-p.x*p.x*2.5);
 float lower=exp(-max(d,0.)*2.5)*smoothstep(-.01,.055,d)*exp(-p.x*p.x*3.6);
 float core=exp(-pow(p.x/.105,2.))*exp(-pow(rimDistance/.012,2.));
 vec3 col=vec3(.08,.22,.53)*upper*1.15;
 col+=vec3(.045,.19,.60)*lower*1.15;
 col+=vec3(.035,.32,.73)*halo*.62;
 col+=vec3(.10,.88,1.)*tight*.56;
 
 // Reverse pointer coordinates to match the conversion layer's 180-degree rotation.
 vec2 pointer=vec2(-uMouse.x*.5*uRes.x/uRes.y,.5-uMouse.y*.5);
 float pointerInfluence=1.-smoothstep(0.,uMouseRadius,length(p-pointer));
 float interaction=1.+uMouseBoost*pointerInfluence;
 col=clamp(col*pulse*interaction,0.,1.);
 // Hover adds an icy white/blue highlight only to the local rim.
 float hoverTint=clamp(uMouseBoost/.4,0.,1.)*pointerInfluence;
 float rimTint=exp(-pow(rimDistance/.05,2.))*hoverTint;
 float lightness=max(col.r,max(col.g,col.b));
 col=mix(col,vec3(.84,.91,1.)*lightness,rimTint*.90);
 col=mix(col,vec3(1.,.97,.88),core*.98);
 gl_FragColor=vec4(col,1.);
}`});i.add(new b.Mesh(new b.PlaneGeometry(2,2),s));var c=C?1000:4200,l=C?.7:1;function T(){var e=(Math.random()+Math.random()+Math.random())/3*2-1;return Math.sign(e)*Math.abs(e)**1.6}for(var u=new Float32Array(c),g=0;g<c;g++)d[g*3]=Math.random()<.96?T()*.60:Math.random()*2-1,d[g*3+1]=-1+Math.random()**.5*2,d[g*3+2]=0,f[g]=Math.random()*100,h[g]=Math.random(),p[g]=.003+h[g]*.023,m[g]=(.65+Math.random()*1.15+h[g]*1.15)*l;u.setAttribute(`position`,new b.BufferAttribute(d,3)),u.setAttribute(`aSeed`,new b.BufferAttribute(f,1)),u.setAttribute(`aSpeed`,new b.BufferAttribute(p,1)),u.setAttribute(`aSize`,new b.BufferAttribute(m,1)),u.setAttribute(`aDepth`,new b.BufferAttribute(h,1));var _={uTime:{value:0},uPixelRatio:{value:Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio||1,C?1:2)},uAspect:{value:1},uNearCol:{value:new b.Vector3(1,1,1)},uFarCol:{value:new b.Vector3(.016,.2,1)},uBlur:{value:.95},uHorizon:{value:.28}},v=new b.ShaderMaterial({transparent:!0,depthTest:!1,blending:b.AdditiveBlending,uniforms:_,vertexShader:`attribute float aSeed,aSpeed,aSize,aDepth;uniform float uTime,uPixelRatio,uAspect,uHorizon;varying float vAlpha,vDepth;void main(){vec3 p=position;float y=mod(p.y+uTime*aSpeed+aSeed,2.)-1.;float x=p.x+sin(uTime*.11+aSeed*6.2831)*0.004*aDepth/.018;float xa=x*uAspect;float dh=uHorizon-xa*xa*0.17;float above=step(dh*2.-1.,y);float tw=.4+.6*(.5+.5*sin(uTime*.9+aSeed*10.));float distanceFromArc=(y+1.)*.5-dh;float hf=smoothstep(.025,.10,distanceFromArc)*exp(-pow((distanceFromArc-.21)/.24,2.));float db=mix(.35,1.,aDepth);float cg=exp(-x*x*3.5);float cb=mix(.45,1.,cg);vAlpha=tw*hf*db*cb*above*.52;vDepth=aDepth;gl_Position=vec4(x,y,0.,1.);gl_PointSize=aSize*uPixelRatio;}`,fragmentShader:`precision mediump float;uniform vec3 uNearCol,uFarCol;uniform float uBlur;varying float vAlpha,vDepth;void main(){vec2 c=gl_PointCoord-.5;float d=length(c);float edge=mix(0.5,mix(0.5,0.05,uBlur),1.-vDepth);float a=smoothstep(0.5,edge,d)*vAlpha;vec3 col=mix(uFarCol,uNearCol,vDepth);gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a);}`}),y=new b.Points(u,v);i.add(y);let E=new b.Vector2,D=new b.Vector2,O=e.closest(`[data-vrn-hero]`),k=!1,A=0,j=0,M=null,N=1,P=1;function F(t){if(S.matches)return;pointerActive=true;let n=e.getBoundingClientRect();E.set((t.clientX-n.left)/n.width*2-1,-(t.clientY-n.top)/n.height*2+1)}function I(){pointerActive=false;E.set(0,0)}function L(){if(t||lost)return;o.uMouseBoost.value+=( (pointerActive&&!S.matches?.4:0)-o.uMouseBoost.value)*.08;o.uTime.value=_.uTime.value=S.matches?0:j,o.uMouse.value.copy(D),w.render(i,a),e.dataset.ready=`true`}function R(){N=e.clientWidth||1,P=e.clientHeight||1;let t=Math.min(devicePixelRatio||1,N<768?1:2);w.setPixelRatio(t),w.setSize(N,P,!1),o.uRes.value.set(N,P),o.uHorizon.value=_.uHorizon.value=N<768?.20:.26,_.uAspect.value=N/P,_.uPixelRatio.value=t,L()}function IntersectionObserver(([e])=>{k=e.isIntersecting,B()},{rootMargin:`100px 0px`});R(),U.observe(e),W.observe(O),O.addEventListener(`pointermove`,F),O.addEventListener(`pointerleave`,I),document.addEventListener(`visibilitychange`,B),S.addEventListener(`change`,B),x.addEventListener(`webglcontextlost`,V),x.addEventListener(`webglcontextrestored`,H),n=()=>{cancelAnimationFrame(A),W.disconnect(),U.disconnect(),O.removeEventListener(`pointermove`,F),O.removeEventListener(`pointerleave`,I),document.removeEventListener(`visibilitychange`,B),S.removeEventListener(`change`,B),x.removeEventListener(`webglcontextlost`,V),x.removeEventListener(`webglcontextrestored`,H),i.children.forEach(e=>{e.geometry.dispose(),e.material.dispose()}),w.dispose(),x.remove()};api.particleCount=C?1000:4200;e.dataset.particles=String(api.particleCount);api.contextLossTest=()=>w.getContext().getExtension("WEBGL_lose_context");return api;}catch(error){console.warn("Varonis hero renderer unavailable; static fallback remains visible.",error);n();e.dataset.animating="false";e.dataset.error="renderer";e.querySelector("canvas")?.remove();delete e.dataset.ready;return api;}}

if(!window.VaronisConversionGlow){
 const apis=new Map(),near=new Set();let generation=0,observer=null;
 async function start(host){
  if(document.hidden||apis.get(host)!==null||!host.isConnected)return;
  const current=generation;apis.set(host,{pending:true});
  const api=await initHero(host);
  if(current!==generation||!host.isConnected){api?.destroy();return;}
  apis.set(host,api);
 }
 function init(){
  if(!observer&&'IntersectionObserver' in window)observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
   for(const entry of entries){const host=entry.target;if(entry.isIntersecting){near.add(host);start(host);}else near.delete(host);}
  },{rootMargin:'300px 0px'});
  for(const host of document.querySelectorAll('[data-conversion-glow]')){
   if(host.closest('[data-wf--conversion-panel--variant="about-us"]'))continue;
   if(apis.has(host))continue;apis.set(host,null);
   if(observer)observer.observe(host);else{near.add(host);start(host);}
  }
 }
 function resume(){if(!document.hidden)near.forEach(start);}
 function destroy(){
  generation++;observer?.disconnect();observer=null;near.clear();
  for(const [host,api] of apis){if(api?.destroy)api.destroy();else host.__vrnGlow?.destroy();}
  apis.clear();
 }
 window.VaronisConversionGlow={init,destroy};
 window.addEventListener('pagehide',destroy);window.addEventListener('pageshow',init);
 document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',resume);
}
window.VaronisConversionGlow.init();

</script>

<script>
(()=>{
function init(){
const main=document.querySelector('.blog-hub'),grid=main?.querySelector('[data-blog-grid]'),list=grid?.querySelector(':scope>.w-dyn-items');
if(!main||!list||main.dataset.blogReady)return;
const initial=[...list.children].filter(n=>n.querySelector('.blog-card-title')?.textContent.trim());
if(!initial.length){new MutationObserver((m,o)=>{if(list.querySelector('.blog-card-title')?.textContent.trim()){o.disconnect();init()}}).observe(list,{subtree:true,childList:true});return}
main.dataset.blogReady='true';
const hero=main.querySelector('.blog-hero-1');
if(hero&&!hero.querySelector('.blog-rulers'))hero.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<div class="blog-rulers" aria-hidden="true"><div class="blog-ruler blog-ruler-left"><img alt="" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6ab177fe95f1db26a30464e3/6ab2bd207fff80d924a9ab4c_partnerships-hero-ruler.svg"></div><div class="blog-ruler blog-ruler-right"><img alt="" src="https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6ab177fe95f1db26a30464e3/6ab2bd837ae2196514f376f0_partnerships-hero-ruler-right.svg"></div></div>');
const search=main.querySelector('.blog-search-wrap .input'),filters=main.querySelector('.blog-filters-wrap>.events-formats'),filterWrap=filters.parentElement,empty=main.querySelector('.blog-no-results');let more=main.querySelector('.blog-more-wrap>.button');
function nextPage(root){const a=root.querySelector('.w-pagination-next'),href=a?.getAttribute('href');return href&&href!=='#'&&a.style.display!=='none'&&!a.hidden?href:null}
let topic='All',visible=12,cells=initial,next=nextPage(grid),loading=false,loadError=false,filterTimer;
const text=(el,selector)=>el.querySelector(selector)?.textContent.trim()||'';
const clean=el=>{el.removeAttribute('id');el.querySelectorAll('[id]').forEach(n=>n.removeAttribute('id'));return el};
function prepare(cell){
 const card=cell.querySelector('.blog-card');if(!card)return;card.querySelectorAll('img').forEach(img=>img.setAttribute('loading','lazy'));
 card.querySelectorAll('.blog-card-primary,.blog-secondary-label').forEach(n=>{if(!n.textContent.trim())n.hidden=true});
 const secondary=card.querySelector('.blog-card-secondary');if(secondary&&!secondary.textContent.trim())secondary.hidden=true;
 const people=[...card.querySelectorAll('.blog-author')].filter(p=>p.textContent.trim());
 if(people.length>2&&!card.querySelector('.blog-author-summary')){const wrap=card.querySelector('.blog-card-authors'),summary=document.createElement('div'),portraits=document.createElement('div'),label=document.createElement('span');summary.className='blog-author-summary';portraits.className='blog-author-portraits';people.slice(0,2).forEach(p=>{const img=p.querySelector('img');if(img?.getAttribute('src'))portraits.append(clean(img.cloneNode(true)))});const count=document.createElement('span');count.className='blog-author-count';count.textContent=''+(people.length-2)+'+';portraits.append(count);label.textContent=people.slice(0,2).map(p=>p.textContent.trim()).join(', ')+', and '+(people.length-2)+' others';summary.append(portraits,label);wrap.children[0].hidden=true;wrap.append(summary)}
 cell.dataset.search=(text(card,'.blog-card-title')+' '+text(card,'.blog-card-excerpt')+' '+text(card,'.blog-card-tags')+' '+text(card,'.blog-card-authors')).toLocaleLowerCase();
 cell.dataset.topics=JSON.stringify([...card.querySelectorAll('.blog-card-primary,.blog-secondary-label')].map(n=>n.textContent.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()));
}
cells.forEach(prepare);[...list.children].filter(n=>!cells.includes(n)).forEach(n=>n.hidden=true);search.setAttribute('placeholder','Search...');search.setAttribute('aria-label','Search blog posts');const searchLabel=search.closest('.events-search-wrap')?.querySelector('label');if(searchLabel)searchLabel.textContent='Search blog posts';const searchClear=search.closest('.events-search-wrap')?.querySelector('.events-clear');if(searchClear){searchClear.setAttribute('aria-label','Clear blog search');const syncClear=()=>{const on=!!search.value;searchClear.classList.toggle('is-visible',on);searchClear.disabled=!on;searchClear.setAttribute('aria-hidden',String(!on))};search.addEventListener('input',syncClear);searchClear.addEventListener('click',()=>{search.value='';search.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));commitSearch();search.focus()});requestAnimationFrame(syncClear);}
function matches(cell){return (!search.value.trim()||cell.dataset.search.includes(search.value.trim().toLocaleLowerCase()))&&(topic==='All'||JSON.parse(cell.dataset.topics).includes(topic.toLocaleLowerCase()))}
function matchCount(){return cells.filter(matches).length}
function render(){let count=0;cells.forEach(cell=>{const match=matches(cell);cell.hidden=!match||count>=visible;if(match)count++});empty.hidden=count!==0||loading;more.parentElement.hidden=count<=visible&&!next;more.textContent=loading?'Loading posts…':loadError?'Try loading more posts':'Show more posts';more.setAttribute('aria-disabled',String(loading));more.disabled=loading;list.setAttribute('aria-busy',String(loading))}
function requestFiltered(){clearTimeout(filterTimer);filterTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(topic!=='All'||search.value.trim())loadNext(true)},250)}

function buttonize(el){if(el.tagName==='BUTTON')return el;const b=document.createElement('button');[...el.attributes].forEach(a=>{if(a.name!=='href')b.setAttribute(a.name,a.value)});b.type='button';b.replaceChildren(...el.childNodes);el.replaceWith(b);return b}
const topicKey=value=>String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'');
const topicButtons=[...filters.querySelectorAll('[data-topic]')].map(buttonize);
function selectTopic(value,writeUrl=false){
 const selected=topicButtons.find(b=>topicKey(b.dataset.topic)===topicKey(value))||topicButtons.find(b=>b.dataset.topic==='All');
 topic=selected?.dataset.topic||'All';visible=12;
 topicButtons.forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(b===selected)));
 if(writeUrl){const url=new URL(location.href);if(topic==='All')url.searchParams.delete('topic');else url.searchParams.set('topic',topicKey(topic));history.replaceState(null,'',url);}
 render();requestFiltered();
}
topicButtons.forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>selectTopic(b.dataset.topic,true)));
selectTopic(new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('topic'));
window.addEventListener('popstate',()=>{const url=new URL(location.href);search.value=url.searchParams.get('q')||'';search.dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}));selectTopic(url.searchParams.get('topic'))});
const searchForm=search.closest('form');
search.value=new URL(location.href).searchParams.get('q')||'';
render();
function commitSearch(){
 const url=new URL(location.href),query=search.value.trim();
 if(query)url.searchParams.set('q',query);else url.searchParams.delete('q');
 history.replaceState(null,'',url);
 visible=12;render();requestFiltered();
}
searchForm?.addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();commitSearch()},true);
search.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.isComposing){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();commitSearch()}},true);
search.addEventListener('input',()=>{visible=12;render();requestFiltered()});
const moreButton=buttonize(more);more=moreButton;moreButton.addEventListener('click',async()=>{if(loading)return;if(!loadError)visible+=12;await loadNext(true);render();const shown=cells.filter(c=>!c.hidden);shown[Math.max(0,shown.length-12)]?.querySelector('a')?.focus({preventScroll:true})});
function edge(){filterWrap.toggleAttribute('data-left',filters.scrollLeft>2);filterWrap.toggleAttribute('data-right',filters.scrollLeft+filters.clientWidth<filters.scrollWidth-2)}filters.addEventListener('scroll',edge,{passive:true});new ResizeObserver(edge).observe(filters);edge();
async function loadNext(fillMatches){
 if(!next||loading||matchCount()>=visible)return;
 loading=true;loadError=false;render();
 try{
  do{
   const url=new URL(next,location.href);
   if(url.origin!==location.origin)throw Error('Unexpected pagination origin');
   const response=await fetch(url);
   if(!response.ok)throw Error('Page unavailable');
   const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(await response.text(),'text/html'),incoming=doc.querySelector('[data-blog-grid]'),incomingList=incoming?.querySelector(':scope>.w-dyn-items');
   if(!incomingList)throw Error('Missing post list');
   const existing=new Set(cells.map(c=>c.querySelector('.blog-card-link')?.getAttribute('href')));
   [...incomingList.children].forEach(c=>{
    const href=c.querySelector('.blog-card-link')?.getAttribute('href');
    if(href&&!existing.has(href)){existing.add(href);const copy=document.importNode(c,true);copy.hidden=true;prepare(copy);list.append(copy);cells.push(copy)}
   });
   const candidate=nextPage(incoming);next=candidate===next?null:candidate;
   render();
  }while(fillMatches&&next&&matchCount()<visible);
 }catch(e){loadError=true}
 finally{loading=false;render();featured()}
}

grid.querySelector('.w-pagination-wrapper')?.classList.add('blog-native-pagination');
grid.querySelectorAll('link[rel="prerender"],link[rel="prefetch"]').forEach(el=>el.remove());
function featured(){const item=main.querySelector('[data-blog-feature-source] .w-dyn-item'),card=item?.querySelector('.blog-card');if(!card||card.dataset.featureReady||!card.querySelector('.blog-card-title')?.textContent.trim())return;prepare(item);card.querySelector('.blog-card-image')?.setAttribute('loading','eager');card.querySelector('.blog-card-image')?.setAttribute('fetchpriority','high');card.dataset.featureReady='true'}featured();
function ticker(){const root=main.querySelector('.blog-ticker');if(!root)return;const selected=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-blog-ticker-source] .w-dyn-item')];selected.forEach(c=>{const a=c.querySelector('[data-ticker-slug]');if(a)a.setAttribute('href','/blog/'+encodeURIComponent(a.dataset.tickerSlug))});if(!selected.length){root.hidden=true;return}const signature=selected.map(c=>c.querySelector('.blog-card-link').getAttribute('href')).join('|');if(root.dataset.ready===signature)return;root.replaceChildren();root.hidden=false;root.dataset.ready=signature;const track=document.createElement('div'),group=document.createElement('div');track.className='blog-ticker-track';group.className='blog-ticker-group';selected.forEach(c=>{const a=document.createElement('a'),badge=document.createElement('span'),date=document.createElement('span'),title=document.createElement('span');a.className='blog-ticker-item';a.href=c.querySelector('.blog-card-link').getAttribute('href');badge.className='blog-ticker-new';badge.textContent='New';date.className='blog-ticker-date';date.textContent=text(c,'[data-blog-updated]')||text(c,'.blog-card-date');title.textContent=text(c,'.blog-card-title');a.append(badge,date,title);group.append(a)});track.append(group);root.append(track);function size(){[...track.children].slice(1).forEach(n=>n.remove());const width=group.getBoundingClientRect().width;if(!width)return;track.style.setProperty('--ticker-distance',width+'px');track.style.setProperty('--ticker-duration',width/28+'s');for(let i=0;i<Math.ceil(root.clientWidth/width)+1;i++){const copy=group.cloneNode(true);copy.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');copy.setAttribute('inert','');track.append(copy)}}new ResizeObserver(size).observe(root);document.fonts.ready.then(size);size()}ticker();
const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>{featured();ticker()});const featureSource=main.querySelector('[data-blog-feature-source]');if(featureSource)observer.observe(featureSource,{childList:true,subtree:true});
render();requestFiltered();
}
let queued=false;const boot=new MutationObserver(()=>{if(queued)return;queued=true;requestAnimationFrame(()=>{queued=false;init();if(document.querySelector('.blog-hub')?.dataset.blogReady)boot.disconnect()})});boot.observe(document.documentElement,{subtree:true,childList:true});if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true});else init();
})();
</script>


<script>
/* Use the same contour module and scroll response as the site's other pages. */
(()=>{
async function init(){
const hero=document.querySelector('.blog-hero-1'),edge=document.querySelector('.blog-conversion-edge'),shell=document.querySelector('.blog-conversion-shell');
if(!hero||!edge||!shell||hero.dataset.blogMotion)return;
try{
const {t:contour}=await import('https://cdn.prod.website-files.com/6aad3cd63e81c645c771a0be/6aad67f484890358c783f906_varonis-contours.v2.js');
if(hero.dataset.blogMotion)return;
hero.dataset.blogMotion='true';hero.dataset.contour='bottom';
const hs=document.createElement('div');hs.className='blog-hero-motion-surface';hs.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');hero.prepend(hs);
const cta=document.createElement('section');cta.className='blog-cta-motion';cta.id='demo';cta.dataset.navTheme='dark';shell.before(cta);cta.append(edge,shell);
const cs=document.createElement('div');cs.className='blog-cta-motion-surface';cs.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');cta.prepend(cs);
let cleanups=[];
let frame=0;
const mobile=matchMedia('(max-width:767px)');
function start(){cleanups.forEach(fn=>fn?.());cleanups=[contour(hs),contour(cs)];}
function refresh(){frame=0;start();}
function schedule(){if(mobile.matches&&!frame)frame=requestAnimationFrame(refresh);}
start();
// Capture mobile scrolling, including browser toolbar and restored-page changes.
addEventListener('scroll',schedule,{passive:true,capture:true});
visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize',schedule,{passive:true});
addEventListener('orientationchange',schedule);
addEventListener('pageshow',start);
addEventListener('pagehide',()=>{cancelAnimationFrame(frame);frame=0;cleanups.forEach(fn=>fn?.());cleanups=[];});
}catch(error){console.warn('Blog contour unavailable; static artwork retained.',error)}
}
document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true}):init();
})();
</script>
<script>
/* Blog chrome follows the light archive and dark animated sections. */
(()=>{
function init(){
const hero=document.querySelector('.blog-hero-1'),results=document.querySelector('.blog-results'),header=document.querySelector('.site-header'),nav=document.querySelector('.site-nav-shell');
if(!hero||!results||!header||!nav||header.dataset.blogThemeReady)return;
header.dataset.blogThemeReady='true';let frame=0;
function update(){
frame=0;
const probe=nav.getBoundingClientRect().bottom;
const depth=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(hero).getPropertyValue('--hero-notch-buffer'))||0;
const cta=document.querySelector('.blog-cta-motion')||document.querySelector('.blog-conversion-edge');
const light=probe>=hero.getBoundingClientRect().bottom-depth&&(!cta||probe<cta.getBoundingClientRect().top);
header.classList.toggle('cc-varonis-light',light);header.dataset.navState=light?'light':'dark';header.dataset.navThemeReady='true';
header.querySelectorAll('.nav-panel').forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('cc-varonis-light',light));
header.querySelectorAll('.content-image,.nav-end .button,.button-image').forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('cc-nav-light',light));
header.querySelectorAll('.nav-language-list').forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('cc-light',light));
}
const schedule=()=>{if(!frame)frame=requestAnimationFrame(update)};
addEventListener('scroll',schedule,{passive:true});addEventListener('resize',schedule);
new ResizeObserver(schedule).observe(document.body);update();
}
document.readyState==='loading'?document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',init,{once:true}):init();
})();
</script>

<script>(()=>{const init=()=>{document.querySelectorAll('.product-component [role="tablist"]').forEach(list=>{if(list.dataset.tickReady)return;list.dataset.tickReady='true';let <style id="accordion-divider-stacking">details.accordion-component[open]{z-index:1}</style>
<!-- Consent preferences: interruption-safe disclosure motion and keyboard-only toggle focus. -->
<script>
/* Native Consent Pro owns state; CSS owns motion. Wrappers allow auto-height
   disclosures without per-frame measurements and keep rapid reversal continuous. */
(()=>{
 const seen=new WeakSet();
 function mount(root){
  if(seen.has(root))return;seen.add(root);
  let keyboard=false;
  root.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Tab')keyboard=true;},true);
  root.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>{keyboard=false;root.querySelectorAll('[data-consent-keyboard]').forEach(e=>e.removeAttribute('data-consent-keyboard'));},true);
  root.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{const field=e.target.closest?.('.consent_prefs_checkbox-field');if(field&&keyboard)field.setAttribute('data-consent-keyboard','');},true);
  root.addEventListener('focusout',e=>e.target.closest?.('.consent_prefs_checkbox-field')?.removeAttribute('data-consent-keyboard'),true);
  // Capture before native state changes; animate frame and disclosures together.
  const reduced=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)');
  let pending=null, timer=0, raf=0, active=[];
  function clearMotion(){
   cancelAnimationFrame(raf);clearTimeout(timer);
   active.forEach(el=>{for(const key of ['height','transition','grid-template-rows','visibility'])el.style.removeProperty(key);});
   active=[];
  }
  function capture(e){
   const trigger=e.target.closest?.('[fs-consent-element="accordion-trigger"]');
   if(!trigger|| (e.type==='keydown'&&!['Enter',' '].includes(e.key)))return;
   const form=trigger.closest('.consent_prefs_form');
   if(!form||reduced.matches)return;
   const panels=[...form.querySelectorAll('[data-consent-motion]')];
   const elements=[form,...panels];
   const heights=elements.map(el=>el.getBoundingClientRect().height);
   clearMotion();
   pending={form,elements,heights,opening:trigger.getAttribute("aria-expanded")!=="true"};
  }
  root.addEventListener('click',capture,true);
  root.addEventListener('keydown',capture,true);
  new MutationObserver(records=>{
   if(!pending||!records.some(r=>r.target.matches?.('[data-consent-motion]')))return;
   const {form,elements,heights,opening}=pending;pending=null;
   if(reduced.matches||!form.getBoundingClientRect().height)return;
   // Resolve natural end geometry once; never measure on animation frames.
   elements.forEach(el=>el.style.setProperty('transition','none'));
   const targets=elements.map(el=>el.getBoundingClientRect().height);
   const duration=opening?320:200;
   active=elements;
   elements.forEach((el,i)=>{
    el.style.height=heights[i]+'px';
    if(el!==form){el.style.gridTemplateRows='1fr';el.style.visibility='visible';}
   });
   void form.offsetHeight;
   raf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
    elements.forEach((el,i)=>{el.style.transition=`height ${duration}ms cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)`;el.style.height=targets[i]+'px';});
    timer=setTimeout(clearMotion,duration+40);
   });
  }).observe(root,{subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  reduced.addEventListener('change',()=>{pending=null;clearMotion();});
  window.addEventListener('resize',()=>{pending=null;clearMotion();},{passive:true});

  function prepare(){
   root.querySelectorAll('.consent_prefs_form [fs-consent-element="accordion-content"]').forEach(panel=>{
    if(panel.hasAttribute('data-consent-motion'))return;
    const inner=document.createElement('div');inner.className='consent-motion-inner';
    inner.append(...panel.childNodes);panel.append(inner);panel.setAttribute('data-consent-motion','');
    const sync=()=>{inner.inert=!panel.classList.contains('is-active');};sync();
    new MutationObserver(sync).observe(panel,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
   });
  }
  new MutationObserver(prepare).observe(root,{childList:true,subtree:true});prepare();
 }
 function discover(){for(const host of document.querySelectorAll('body > *'))if(host.shadowRoot)mount(host.shadowRoot);}
 new MutationObserver(discover).observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});discover();
})();

</script>
<!-- Consent Pro: use the shared footer link instead of the floating preferences control. -->
<style id="consent-footer-preferences-trigger">consentpro-fixed-preferences{display:none!important}</style>
<script>
window.FinsweetConsentPro=window.FinsweetConsentPro||[];
window.FinsweetConsentPro.push(['consent',function(consent){
 const hideFloatingPreferences=()=>consent.elements.fixedPreferences.hide();
 hideFloatingPreferences();
 consent.on('fixed-preferences-shown',hideFloatingPreferences);
}]);
</script>
<!-- CTA: Atlas demo in the standard Wistia JavaScript player. -->
<script id="cta-atlas-wistia">
(() => {
  const panel = document.querySelector('[data-conversion-panel]');
  const root = panel?.querySelector('.conversion-player');
  const frame = root?.querySelector('.demo-player-frame');
  if (!frame) return;
  const shell = document.createElement('div');
  shell.className = 'demo-player-frame';
  shell.style.cssText = 'position:relative;width:100%;height:auto;aspect-ratio:16/9;';
  const player = document.createElement('div');
  player.className = 'wistia_embed wistia_async_6hufkbgqzm seo=false autoPlay=false controlsVisibleOnLoad=true playButton=true smallPlayButton=true playbar=true volumeControl=true settingsControl=true fullscreenButton=true playerColor=1746e0 preload=metadata videoFoam=true doNotTrack=true resumable=false';
  player.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Varonis Atlas demo');
  player.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;';
  shell.append(player);
  frame.replaceWith(shell);
  const config = panel.querySelector('[data-conversion-config="player"]');
  if (config) config.textContent = 'https://fast.wistia.com/embed/iframe/6hufkbgqzm';
})();
</script>
